- **ensuring-cross-platform**: Apply platform-agnostic engineering rules so Langflow code, tests, and Docker images behave identically on Ubuntu, macOS (Intel x86_64 + Apple Silicon arm64), and native Windows — plus the linux/amd64 + linux/arm64 Docker matrix and the separate Langflow Desktop distribution for Windows and macOS.
